### [2] git-patch | should-fix | Section 8, the deb-builds trap, against step 6 row deb and section 5.3 (the deb recipe)
QUOTE: The target deb's version carries the local branch name (so the campaign's preflight can tell it from a nightly deb); the base deb is built detached and gets a nightly-looking version (2026.10.10-72308ec9): the campaign's EXPECTED_BASE knobs must name it.
PROBLEM: This contradicts the recipe the executor is told to run. Row deb builds the base "in /var/tmp/jhan/tron-pr4ci-base on the local branch jhan-pr4ci-base", and section 5.3 says the worktree is "put on a local branch with `git checkout -q -B <branch>`, so that bin/get-version.sh appends the branch name to the deb version (2026.10.<day>-<8 chars>-jhan-pr4ci-<arm>)". On that branch the base deb's version is <build date>-72308ec9-jhan-pr4ci-base, not 2026.10.10-72308ec9. The date part is the build day, not 2026-10-10, in both forms. The executor cannot tell which form EXPECTED_BASE must carry without running the build.
EVIDENCE: `git show 72308ec90c:bin/get-version.sh`: `verstr=$(date +"%Y.%m.%d")-$(git rev-parse --short=8 HEAD)` and `elif [[ -n $branch && $branch != "main" ]]; then verstr=$verstr-$branch`; exec/pr4424ci-20261009/build-target.sh:26 (BRANCH=jhan-pr4424ci-target) and :45 (`git -C "$WT" checkout -q -B "$BRANCH"`); exec/results/pr4424ci-20261009/preflight.txt:7 ("version=2026.10.09-667b2ce5-jhan-pr4424ci-target"); exec/pr4424ci-20261009/campaign.sh:415 refuses only the installed version at preflight when it contains "jhan", and :418 takes the base arm's identity from EXPECTED_BASE and EXPECTED_BASE_SHA.
FIX: Replace the sentence with: "Both debs are built on a local branch (jhan-pr4ci-base, jhan-pr4ci-target), so both versions end in the branch name: <build day>-72308ec9-jhan-pr4ci-base and <build day>-<8 chars of COMMIT>-jhan-pr4ci-target (bin/get-version.sh appends the branch name to the date-and-sha version on any branch other than main). The campaign's EXPECTED_BASE knob must carry the base's exact Version field as `dpkg-deb -f base.deb Version` prints it; a base built detached would get a nightly-looking version, which the preflight could mistake for the nightly's package after a crash."
  - refuter: refuted=False :: Problem confirmed, evidence confirmed, proposed fix correct. (1) The quoted sentence is at PR4-handoff.md:489 verbatim. (3) `git show 72308ec90c:bin/get-version.sh` has `verstr=$(date +"%Y.%m.%d")-$(git rev-parse --short=8 HEAD)` and `elif [[ -n $branch && $branch != "main" ]]; then verstr=$verstr-$branch :: CORRECTED_FIX: Replace the sentence at PR4-handoff.md:489 with: "Both debs are built on a local branch (jhan-pr4ci-base, jhan-pr4ci-target), so both versions end in the branch name: <build day>-72308ec9-jhan-pr4ci-base and <build day>-<8 chars of COMMIT>-jhan-pr4ci-target (bin/get-version.sh appends the branch name to the date-and-sha version on any local branch other than main; `make deb` passes that string through bin/get-deb-version.sh, which keeps the hyphens; the date is the day of the build, as `date +%Y.%m.%d` prints it). The campaign's EXPECTED_BASE knob must carry the base's exact Version field as `dpkg-deb -f base.deb Version` prints it (launch.sh:32 and dut.sh compare for equality); a base built detached would get a nightly-looking version (<build day>-72308ec9), which the preflight (campaign.sh:414-415) would accept as the nightly's package and take as the restore target after a crash."

Quoted text: present verbatim at PR4-handoff.md:489, in section 8 ("## 8. Traps", line 483), the "The deb builds" item.

The contradiction is real. Row deb of step 6 (PR4-handoff.md:314) says the base is built "in /var/tmp/jhan/tron-pr4ci-base on the local branch jhan-pr4ci-base". Section 5.3 (PR4-handoff.md:400) says each worktree is put on a local branch with `git checkout -q -B <branch>` so that the version becomes "2026.10.<day>-<8 chars>-jhan-pr4ci-<arm>". The trap at line 489 says the base is built detached with version 2026.10.10-72308ec9. The executor gets two different recipes for the same deb and two different EXPECTED_BASE values.

Evidenc :: CORRECTED_FIX: Replace the sentence "The target deb's version carries the local branch name (so the campaign's preflight can tell it from a nightly deb); the base deb is built detached and gets a nightly-looking version (2026.10.10-72308ec9): the campaign's EXPECTED_BASE knobs must name it." at PR4-handoff.md:489 with:

"Both debs are built on a local branch (jhan-pr4ci-base, jhan-pr4ci-target), as row deb of step 6 and section 5.3 say. bin/get-version.sh appends the branch name to the date-and-sha version on any branch other than main and release-*. So the base's version is <build day>-72308ec9-jhan-pr4ci-base and the target's is <build day>-<8 chars of COMMIT>-jhan-pr4ci-target. The string 2026.10.10-72308ec9 is the Version line of runtron.main1010 (the detached runtron build of 2026-10-10), not a deb version: do not use it. The campaign's EXPECTED_BASE knob must carry the base's exact Version field as `dpkg-deb -f base.deb Version` prints it (launch.sh:32 and dut.sh ensure-base compare that string). A base built detached would get a nightly-looking version, and the preflight could then mistake it for the nightly's package after a crash."

QUOTE: the FPGA holds the K of every complete GOF and the CPU scores only the tail above them
PROBLEM: These terms are used without a definition at first use: GOF (section 6.2); HBM (edit 12, section 5.5, section 6); Dice, local-shared-dice, DICE_DISABLE (5.3 route); DUT (step 6 row W, 5.3); sd (5.5, "sd 4.6 to 8.4 TPS"); engagement point (6.7); shard (6.7); ctest manifest (bench); uv and ghcup (deb recipe); fourmolu (Words, Lint); HF in HF_OFFLINE (section 8); book (step 5.2, section 8); tag and per-tag folders (section 2, 5.4); engine (section 1, 5.5); S in "PR-3's tested S tree" (section 2) and "the A patch" (step 1.4); HugetlbPages (section 6 Words rows); REST PATCH and LF (step 8); relevant token (step 5.2); operation as in cached_operation_uses_hw (Words, Hardware-attention policy).

HBM: the FPGA card's high-bandwidth memory; 'HBM bypass space exhausted' and 'lose HW attention' are runtron warnings that a 1024-token shard was scored on the CPU instead of the card. Shard: a 1024-token slice of a prompt that the FPGA scores. Engagement point: the query position (127) from which the FPGA scores. Dice: the shared FPGA scheduling service that `bin/slice route` may require (local-shared-dice); DICE_DISABLE=1 bypasses it and is forbidden. DUT: device under test, delphi-3bda in the W scripts. sd: sample standard deviation. ctest: CMake's test runner; its manifest lists the test binaries the bench runs. uv: the Python package tool; ghcup: the Haskell toolchain installer; both are on PATH for `make deb`. fourmolu: the Haskell formatter. HF: Hugging Face, the tokenizer source; HF_OFFLINE=1 reads the local cache only. Book: tron's per-request KV bookkeeping object that owns a KV slot. Tag: one driver run of W, named <arm>-pass<k> or <arm>-sw8k-pass<k>; its results folder. Engine: one rinzler or runtron process serving one model placement. S: the letter split-plan.md uses for PR-3's content; the A patch: ref/PR2-diff-76a0..4d3a.patch, PR-2's content. HugetlbPages: the per-process hugepage field of /proc/<pid>/status. REST PATCH: GitHub's HTTP edit call for a PR body; LF: line-feed line endings. Relevant token: a cached token the query attends to. Operation: one cached attention operation of a layer (cached_operation_uses_hw says whether the FPGA scores it)."
  - refuter: refuted=False :: The problem is real. The quoted sentence is at PR4-handoff.md line 452. None of the listed terms appears in the "Words used here" section (lines 9-52): a regex search of that section for GOF, HBM, Dice, DUT, sd, engagement, shard, ctest, ghcup, HF, book, tag, engine, HugetlbPages, REST, LF, relevant, route and local-legacy returns zero hits (only "uv", "fourmolu" and "operation" occur there, each used without a definition). The cited lines exist and use the terms as claimed: GOF only at line 452; HBM at 292, 342, 457; Dice, local-shared-dice and DICE_DISABLE at 392; DUT at 402; "sd 4.6 to 8.4 TPS" at 427; engagement point and shard at 457; ctest manifest at 320 and 401; uv and ghcup at 400 a :: CORRECTED_FIX: Add to "Words used here" (PR4-handoff.md after line 52), replacing the proposed GOF, book, operation and engagement-point entries and keeping the rest:

- GOF: Group of Four, the hardware KV cache's atomic unit of 4 tokens of K and V (gof_tokens = 4 in h/tron/gof.hpp; doc/hw-kv-design.md). gof::populate stages a GOF from the pages to the FPGA card; wait_for_coop_gof waits at the forward end for that cooperative staging. "Every complete GOF" = every full 4-token group; the tail above them is the 1 to 3 newest tokens the CPU scores.
- Shard: a 1024-token slice of a sequence that the FPGA scores (shard_tokens = 8 hardware pages x 128 positions, h/tron/gof.hpp).
- HBM: the FPGA card's high-bandwidth memory, where the card holds the K/V of its shards. "HBM bypass space exhausted; caller degrades to SW attention" (src/pos/driver.cpp) and "... slots lose HW attention" (src/tron/gof.cpp) are warnings in the engine log that a shard's K/V did not fit and the CPU scored that token range instead; campaign W counts both per run.
- Engagement point: the first 0-based sequence position the FPGA scores (hw_attn_engagement_point in h/tron/models/hw_attn_config.hpp). Default 127 (MIN_TOK_IDX_FOR_HWATTN); USE_HW_ATTN=N raises it, rounded up to a page boundary. The CPU scores every position below it.
- Operation: one attention operation of the execution schedule (attention_op_id in h/tron/models/common.hpp). Its id is deliberately distinct from the model layer number and from the KV slot. cached_operation_uses_hw(operation) says whether the FPGA scores that operation's cached K.
- Book: the KV cache's unit of memory allocation (h/tron/models/kv_cache.hpp, the comment before Note [KV slot retention]): it owns a set of pages and their K/V storage, and a page is a lightweight reference into its book. A book may hold no active KV slot.
- Relevant token: a cached token position the query attends to (inside its window and not masked); "the first relevant token" of a page is where the software loop fetches the page's blocks mask and plane.
- Dice: the per-host daemon that arbitrates FPGA box resources across submitters (doc/dice.md). `bin/slice route` answers local-legacy (no Dice), local-shared-dice (submit through the shared Dice service) or distributed-dice. DICE_DISABLE=1 bypasses the service and is forbidden (AGENTS.md).
- DUT: device under test = delphi-3bda in the W scripts (exec/pr4424ci-20261009/README.md).
- Tag: one driver run of W and its results subfolder, named <arm>-pass<k> (FPGA attention) or <arm>-sw8k-pass<k> (the sw8k cell); "per-tag folders" are those subfolders.
- Engine: one serving process for one model placement: a rinzler process (platformd starts them as default-0 .. default-3, one per rinzler@N unit) or, in the runtron records, one runtron process.
- sd: the sample standard deviation (statistics.stdev in summarize.py); in the records cited at section 5.5, the spread of one arm's repetitions in TPS.
- ctest: CMake's test runner. The ctest manifest: the list of test binaries and variants of the native build tree (gen/) that `bin/slice bench` reads (bin/slice_bench_manifest.py); `make build-test` produces it.
- uv: the Python package and environment tool (/tools/uv/0.9.5). ghcup: the Haskell toolchain installer (~/.ghcup/bin). Both are on PATH for `make deb`.
- fourmolu: the Haskell source formatter that Lint runs; not run here because PR-4 changes no Haskell file.
- HF: Hugging Face, the source of the tokenizers. HF_OFFLINE=1 sets HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 for the driver, so every tokenizer must be in the local cache.
- HugetlbPages: a line of /proc/<pid>/status, the memory mapped from 1 GiB hugepages (the weights and the K/V arena); VmRSS and VmHWM are the resident size and its peak.
- S, A: the letters split-plan.md uses for the concerns: A = the AMX kernel on the VNNI plane (PR-2's content), S = the shared block save (PR-3's content), T = the row-major tail block (PR-4's content). "PR-3's tested S tree" = the tree of PR-3's content as tested on 2026-10-08. The A patch: ref/PR2-diff-76a0..4d3a.patch, the PR 4424 diff of concern A (ref/A-diff.patch is a different file; use the one line 193 names).
- REST PATCH: GitHub's HTTP edit call that stores a PR body; it stores LF (line-feed) line endings, so compare bodies after normalizing line endings.
  - refuter: refuted=False :: The problem is real, but the proposed fix carries wrong definitions, so a corrected fix is given.

Quoted text: present verbatim at PR4-handoff.md line 452 (section 6, item 2).

Undefined terms: every listed term is used in the file and none is defined. Verified by grep over PR4-handoff.md: GOF as a noun only at line 452 (gof::populate at 86 and wait_for_coop_gof at 27 are code names, never expanded); HBM at 292, 342, 457; Dice, local-shared-dice, DICE_DISABLE at 392 (the only gloss is "the shared Dice service"); DUT at 402; sd at 427 (three uses; "standard deviation" at line 46 is never linked to "sd"); engagement point and shard at 457; ctest at 320 and 401; uv at 40, 400, 489 and uvx at 1 :: CORRECTED_FIX: Add these rows to "Words used here" (and keep the finding's locations, corrected: DUT is used in section 5.3 only; HBM in edit 12, step 7.2 and section 6.7):

- GOF: group of four, the hardware KV cache's unit: 4 consecutive tokens of K and V (one 8 KB K block and one 8 KB V block). The staging (gof::populate) copies a GOF's rows into a buffer that DMA (direct memory access) moves to the FPGA card. wait_for_coop_gof waits until a pass's cooperative staging has drained. (h/tron/gof.hpp; the Note [Row-major blocks under hardware attention] of PR-4 says the same.)
- Shard: a 1024-token range (8 hardware pages) of one sequence's K and V on one FPGA card, with its HBM ownership (h/tron/shard.hpp, h/tron/gof.hpp shard_tokens).
- HBM: the FPGA card's high-bandwidth memory, which holds the K and V of the shards the card scores. "HBM bypass space exhausted" (src/pos/driver.cpp) and "lose HW attention" (src/tron/gof.cpp) are the warnings a run prints when a shard gets no HBM and its tokens are scored on the CPU instead; campaign.sh counts both per run (campaign.sh:177, 188) and summarize.py shows them per arm row.
- Engagement point: the first sequence position the FPGA scores (hw_attn_engagement_point, 127 by default; USE_HW_ATTN=N raises it, rounded up to a page boundary). The CPU scores every position below it (h/tron/models/hw_attn_config.hpp).
- Dice: tron's per-host queue service for the FPGA cards (doc/dice.md). `bin/slice route` prints a verdict: local-legacy (the scripts' direct runtron launches are the normal commands), local-shared-dice (submit through Dice), distributed-dice, or a blocked route (stop and report). DICE_DISABLE=1 bypasses Dice and is forbidden (AGENTS.md).
- DUT: device under test = delphi-3bda, the name of the campaign scripts (exec/pr4424ci-20261009/README.md); dut.sh is their helper that runs on the machine.
- sd: sample standard deviation (statistics.stdev in summarize.py), printed next to every mean.
- ctest: CMake's test runner. The ctest manifest: the list of test commands of the build tree gen/ that `bin/slice bench` reads to run each test (bin/slice_bench_manifest.py).
- uv: the Python package and tool runner (/tools/uv/0.9.5 on delphi-3bda; `uvx` runs one tool from a package). ghcup: the Haskell toolchain installer (~/.ghcup/bin). Both are on PATH for `make deb` (build-target.sh:87). fourmolu: the Haskell formatter that Lint runs on ingest/ (GNUmakefile).
- HF: Hugging Face, the tokenizer source of the harness. HF_OFFLINE=1 sets HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 for the driver, so every tokenizer must be in the local cache (campaign.sh:54, 288).
- Book: the KV cache's unit of memory allocation. A book owns the memory of a set of pages and the KV slots of that storage; a page is a reference into a book (kv_cache.hpp file header). A book may have no active KV slot (logical_slot_count() == 0).
- Tag (campaign W): one driver run and its results folder, named <arm>-pass<k> (the 14 configs, FPGA attention as shipped) or <arm>-sw8k-pass<k> (the sw8k cell); eight tags per campaign; PASS_TAGS names the tags of a resume (campaign.sh:14-19, 42, 274-277). The git tag of D7 is a different thing.
- Engine: one rinzler process serving one model placement (platformd's named entries default-0 to default-3; exec/pr4424ci-20261009/README.md:17); under runtron, one runtron process.
- S and A: letters of split-plan.md. S = the shared block save, PR-3's content; A = the AMX dense kernel on the VNNI plane, PR-2's content (split-plan.md section 1 table). The A patch: handoff-material/ref/PR2-diff-76a0..4d3a.patch, PR-2's diff.
- VmHWM, VmRSS, HugetlbPages: fields of /proc/<pid>/status that `dut.sh memsnap` reads per rinzler process: the peak resident set size, the current resident set size, and the 1 GiB hugepage mappings (the weights and the K/V arena) (dut.sh:168-171).
- REST PATCH: the GitHub API call that replaces a PR body. LF: line-feed line endings (\n only).
- Relevant token: a cached K token that a query token attends to; under causal attention, the query's ancestors in the token tree (self_attention.hpp Note [Computing self-attention]; relevant_k_tokens is the count per page visit).
- Operation: one attention operation of the model (attention_op_id, common.hpp); a layer's attention is one operation. cached_operation_uses_hw(operation) says, once per model, whether the FPGA scores that operation's K (model.hpp).

  - Never `git worktree prune` the shared repository from claude-box.
  - After any amend, check `git branch --show-current`.
  - git rerere is active in ~/workspace/tron: apply both patches with `-c rerere.enabled=false`, or a recorded resolution of your own earlier run could apply silently. Read the five files after the apply.
  - lefthook is missing on claude-box: commit with --no-verify and run the checks yourself.
